<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">name: paneui-design</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">description: Design rules and checks for paneui interfaces.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">---</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold"># paneui design</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">paneui is a desktop-density component library: a 13px type scale, translucent fills on opaque</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">surfaces, alpha hairlines, four elevation levels, corners that derive from one </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`--radius`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> and stop at</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">16px, one accent swapped through eight role tokens. These rules keep every screen and every coding</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">agent on that one style. Each rule is a short instruction, the reason, and what to write; the</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">references hold the exact values.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">## Install</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">```bash</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">npx</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> skills</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> add</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> QinYangWang/ui</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> --skill</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> paneui-design</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">```</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">The library itself installs with the shadcn CLI (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`npx shadcn@latest add @paneui/theme`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, then any</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">item). See </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`references/registry.md`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">## Check</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">```bash</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">node</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> .agents/skills/paneui-design/scripts/check.mjs</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">            # wherever the skill was installed</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">node</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> .agents/skills/paneui-design/scripts/check.mjs</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> app</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> src</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">    # only these paths</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">```</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Static checks for most of the rules below (tokens, radius, type, motion, icons, hit areas, copy,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">registry names, stale theme; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`--list`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> names each one). No dependencies; with TypeScript in the project it reads JSX exactly (the native</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">TypeScript 7 compiler has no parser API, so there it falls back to line checks). Exit code 1</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">on errors (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`--strict`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">: warnings too), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`--json`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> for tools, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`--list`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> for the rule ids. Configure it in</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`paneui-design.config.json`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (allow-list a file with a reason, add names that must not appear in copy);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">silence one line with a comment </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`design-check-ignore &#x3C;rule> -- &#x3C;reason>`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> above it.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Some rules need the rendered page: open the screen in a browser (menus open, a row hovered) and paste</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`scripts/radius-probe.js`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> into the console for corners more than 2px off concentric, and</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`scripts/polish-probe.js`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> for small or overlapping hit areas, button padding, image outlines, heading</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">wrap, live numbers, smoothing and compositing hints. Run the check before you commit and fix what it</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">reports; a finding you keep gets an allow-list entry with its reason.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`examples/&#x3C;rule>/good.tsx`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> and </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`bad.tsx`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> show each motion, icon and polish check on the library's own</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">components; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`--self-test`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> checks them against their rule.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">## Rules</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 1. Colour with theme tokens</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Tokens follow the theme, the accent preset and dark mode; palette classes and hex values don't.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Use </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`bg-card`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`text-muted-foreground`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`bg-success-subtle`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`inset-ring-success-border`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`var(--chart-3)`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Never </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`bg-zinc-100`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`text-green-600`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`[#0090ff]`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> or a hex in a component. </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`white`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`black`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> only for scrims.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 2. Put text on a solid in its foreground token</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">The neutral accent's solid is near-white in dark mode, so </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`text-white`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> disappears on it.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`bg-primary text-primary-foreground`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">. Hover a filled</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">control to </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`bg-primary-hover`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, press to </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`bg-primary-active`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, never </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`/90`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 3. Fill for state, surface for panels</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`secondary`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`muted`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> and </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`accent`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> are translucent fills; anything that floats or holds content needs an opaque surface.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Hover, selected and track fills use </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`bg-secondary`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> / </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`bg-accent`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> / </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`bg-foreground/6`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">. Cards, popovers,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">menus and dialogs use </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`bg-card`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`bg-popover`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> or </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`bg-background`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 4. Elevate with shadow tokens, not borders plus shadows</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Each shadow level already carries its own hairline, so a CSS border on top doubles the edge.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`shadow-raised`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> for resting cards, tables, code and controls, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`shadow-surface`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> for popovers and menus,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`shadow-xl`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> for dialogs, drawers and the command palette, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`shadow-well`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> for fields and tracks, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`shadow-chip`</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">for keys and small chips. Dividers inside a surface are </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`border-border`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (an alpha hairline).</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 5. Keep series on the chart palette</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Eight fixed hues read the same under every accent, in both modes, and keep neighbours apart.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Series are </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`var(--chart-1)`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> … </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`var(--chart-8)`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> in order; status colours only where the colour means a</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">status. Never </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`primary`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> in a chart. Legend and tooltip swatches are round dots.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 6. Keep nested radii concentric</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">A child corner that doesn't follow its container's curve reads as a mistake at every radius preset.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Inner radius = outer radius − padding. Put </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`frame-{token} frame-p-{n}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> on the container and</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`rounded-inner`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> on the child; a padded child that holds more rows takes </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`frame-inner`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, never</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`frame-inner`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> and </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`rounded-inner`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> together. Insets of 8px or less are always concentric.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 7. Stay at or under 16px</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Past 16px containers turn into blobs and nested parts can't stay concentric.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Use </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`rounded-sm`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> … </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`rounded-3xl`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; every token clamps at </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`--radius-max`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">. Never </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`rounded-4xl`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`5xl`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> in new</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">code or an arbitrary radius above 1rem. </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`rounded-full`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> only for round things: avatars, dots, switches,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">slider thumbs, badges and tags, icon-only circular buttons, progress bars.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 8. Derive every radius from the base radius</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Presets and the radius slider rescale </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`--radius`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> and the tokens built on it, never literal pixel radii.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Use a token, or </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`rounded-[calc(var(--radius)*k)]`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (clamped with </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`min(…, var(--radius-max))`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> above 2×).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Only 1–2px caret radii, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`rounded-full`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> and </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`rounded-[inherit]`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> stay literal.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 9. Use the UI type scale</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">One compact scale keeps dense screens even; arbitrary sizes drift.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Components use </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`text-ui-2xs`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> 10 · </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`text-ui-xs`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> 11 · </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`text-ui-sm`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> 12 · </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`text-ui`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> 13 · </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`text-ui-lg`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> 14.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Reading copy and page headings use </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`text-sm`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> and up; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`text-3xl`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">+ only for heroes. Never </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`text-[11px]`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 10. Semibold titles, medium labels</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Two weights carry the hierarchy; heavier text shouts in a dense layout.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Titles </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`font-semibold`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, labels and buttons </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`font-medium`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, body </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`font-normal`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">. </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`font-bold`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> only in</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">reading copy that needs it.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 11. Keep the font's tracking</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">The type is spaced for its size; tightening small text makes it harder to read.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`tracking-tight`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> only from </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`text-xl`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> up. Wide tracking only on uppercase labels and shortcut glyphs.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 12. Use tabular numbers for numbers that change or align</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Proportional digits make columns and counters jump.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`tabular-nums`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> on prices, timers, tickers, table columns and counts, not on phone numbers, versions or a</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">count fixed in a sentence. </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`text-balance`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> on headings (never on code), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`text-pretty`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> on paragraphs,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`antialiased`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> once on </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`&#x3C;body>`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (the theme sets it), never per element.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 13. Size controls for desktop density</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Desktop screens hold more per view; touch-sized controls waste it.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Buttons and inputs </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`h-8`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (sm </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`h-7`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, xs </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`h-6`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, lg </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`h-9`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">), menu rows 26px, sidebar rows </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`h-8`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (sub rows</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`h-7`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">), checkbox 16px, switch 32×20, disabled </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`opacity-64`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">. Use component sizes, not heights. Field text stays</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">at the control size on phones too; where iOS focus zoom gets in the way, mount the opt-in </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`IosZoomLock`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> once</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(recommended) or give that field </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`max-md:text-base`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; never turn zoom off for the whole page (check:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`viewport-zoom`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">).</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 14. Space related things closer</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Spacing groups content before any border does.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">4px grid. Icon + label </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`gap-1.5`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">–</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`gap-2`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, control groups </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`gap-2`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">–</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`gap-3`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, card padding </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`p-4`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">–</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`p-6`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">sections </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`gap-6`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">–</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`gap-10`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">. Put space with </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`gap`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> on the parent, never padding on a component.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 15. Start navigation text on one left edge</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Indents and guide lines widen the sidebar and break the column the eye follows.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Section labels, group labels and rows share one text edge; hierarchy comes from type and spacing.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">The active row is a fill (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`bg-secondary`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> or </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`bg-foreground/6`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) with an optional 2px bar in the gutter.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 16. Compose the library, don't restyle it</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">A component's variants and sizes are its design; overriding them per screen forks the style.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Pick a </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`variant`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`size`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`className`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> on a component is for layout (margins, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`w-full`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, grid placement).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">If nothing fits, style a plain wrapper, or add a variant to the component in its own change.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 17. Keep a menu label inside its group</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">The label names the group for assistive tech, and the primitive label throws outside one.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`&#x3C;MenuGroup>&#x3C;MenuLabel>Agent&#x3C;/MenuLabel>…items…&#x3C;/MenuGroup>`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; the same for select and combobox labels.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 18. Chevrons for folders, file icons for files</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">A folder icon next to a chevron says the same thing twice and pushes names apart.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Folders show only the expand chevron; files show their file-type icon in the same column, so names</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">at one level line up.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 19. Draw icons from one family per group</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Two glyph families in one group look like two different products.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Navigation, rails, docks, tab bars and palettes use the nav glyphs (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`InboxIcon`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`CalendarDaysIcon`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`TerminalSquareIcon`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">), outline at rest like the controls beside them. A nav with a row fill (sidebar,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">rail) keeps the outline glyph when active; one without (a tab bar) swaps to the filled pair,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`activeIcon={&#x3C;InboxFilledIcon />}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">. Icons come from </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`@/components/icons`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> only, 16px and muted in</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">navigation.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 20. Frame title icons, keep nav icons bare</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">A frame marks an icon as the subject of a card; in a list it is clutter.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Card, alert and header icons sit in </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`&#x3C;IconChip>`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (one size per list, tone for status). Nav, toolbar</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">and inline icons are bare and take their size from the control.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 21. Use real brand marks for other products</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Redrawn or approximated logos look wrong to anyone who knows the brand.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`&#x3C;BrandIcon icon={siGithub} />`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> with one named import per logo; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`color="brand"`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> where the logo is the</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">content, the default </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`current`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> in toolbars and nav. Brands the set leaves out get a neutral icon.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 22. Show people and workspaces as avatars</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">An avatar says "who"; a generic icon in that spot says nothing.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`&#x3C;Avatar>`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> with an image, a generated face or initials, round for people, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`shape="rounded"`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> for workspaces</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">and teams.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 23. Answer a press with a fill</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Native controls don't shrink when pressed; scaling every button makes the screen feel springy.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Buttons, triggers, toggles, nav rows, checkboxes and radios change fill on </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`:active`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">. Press scale only</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">where the motion is the component's point (hold or slide actions, expanding controls, card stacks).</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 24. Transition named properties, briefly</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`transition-all`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> animates layout you didn't mean to, and slow hovers feel laggy.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`transition-colors`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> or </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`transition-[box-shadow,background-color]`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`duration-150`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> for hover and press,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`200`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> for state changes, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`300`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> for reveals; ease </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1)`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">. Springs stay bounce-free:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`bounce: 0`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, or stiffness 380–520, damping 26–42, mass 0.5–0.7 (never under 0.6 of critical damping).</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 25. Gate movement on reduced motion</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Movement, scale and blur can make some readers ill; an opacity fade carries the same change.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`useReducedMotion()`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`motion-safe:`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> / </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`motion-reduce:`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; under reduce, cross-fade instead of cutting.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Popups enter from </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`scale-98`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> plus opacity; exits are shorter than entrances.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 26. Show a ring on keyboard focus</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Keyboard users need to see where they are; a hover style doesn't help them.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring/50`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> on everything interactive. </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`outline-none`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> only with</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">that ring. Check every change in light and dark.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 27. Let the preview explain</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">A live preview already shows the look, layout and states; text that repeats it is noise.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Overview cards show the name and the preview only. Example blocks are title, preview and code, with at</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">most one lead-in sentence that says when to use it.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 28. Keep descriptions to one plain sentence</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">A registry description is the page lede, the search text and the meta tag at once.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">One sentence of 12 words or fewer, no values, lists or paths. Exact values go in the API table or a note.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 29. Never write colon lists or stacked adjectives</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">"X: a, b, c and d" and "powerful, seamless" read as filler.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Say what the thing does. Drop complete, comprehensive, powerful, seamless, robust, modern, flexible.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 30. Describe what it does, not where it came from</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Copy names the job, not the projects behind it.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">"Block editor", "the syntax highlighter", "desktop density". No </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`inspired by`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`port of`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> or </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`X-style`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> in</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">comments, copy or docs; licence notices live in one notices file. Package names stay where they are</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">needed: imports, dependencies, install commands.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 31. Name template parts apart from registry items</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">A part file named like an item (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`parts/avatar.tsx`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> next to the </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`avatar`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> item) collides on install and in imports.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Name parts for their job: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`parts/author-avatar.tsx`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`parts/buffers.ts`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 32. Run the upgrade check after a theme update</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">The CLI adds and updates theme variables but never deletes one, so removed tokens linger.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`npx shadcn@latest add @paneui/upgrade-check`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, then </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`node scripts/paneui-upgrade-check.mjs`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`--fix`</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">cleans the stylesheet). The design check runs it for you when it is installed.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 33. Give every target 24px to land on</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">A 16px checkbox or close button is hard to hit with a trackpad and harder with a finger.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Keep the visible size and extend the target with a pseudo-element: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`relative after:absolute after:-inset-1`</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">takes 16px to 24px, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`pointer-coarse:after:-inset-3.5`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> to 44px on touch. Shrink the extension where it</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">would reach a neighbour; two targets never share pixels. Checkbox and radio already do this.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 34. Trim the icon side of a button</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">An icon's side bearing adds space, so equal padding pushes the content off centre.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Mark the glyph </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`data-icon="inline-start"`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> or </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`"inline-end"`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; Button takes 2px off that side at every</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">size. House icons are drawn optically centred (the play triangle sits right of centre), so don't</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">nudge them with margins.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 35. Outline images with a neutral hairline</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Without an edge a pale photo melts into a white card; a grey border picks up the surface tint.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`media-outline`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> on </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`img`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> and </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`video`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">: a 1px inset line, black at 10%, white at 10% in dark. Leave it</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">off transparent marks such as favicons and logos.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 36. Cross-fade icon swaps</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">A glyph that cuts from copy to check is easy to miss; one that changes in place reads as a new state.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Both glyphs move between </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`opacity 0, scale 0.25, blur 4px`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> and </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`opacity 1, scale 1, blur 0`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> on</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`{ type: "spring", duration: 0.3, bounce: 0 }`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, inside </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`&#x3C;AnimatePresence initial={false} mode="popLayout">`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`&#x3C;ActionSwapBlurIcon value>`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> is that recipe as a component. A library file without motion keeps both</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">glyphs in one grid cell and toggles </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`opacity-0 motion-safe:scale-25 motion-safe:blur-xs`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 37. Transition states, keyframe one-offs</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">A transition retargets when a state flips mid-way; a keyframe restarts and snaps.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Hover, open, checked and pressed use transitions; keyframes only for loops and one-time sequences.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Hover answers within 150ms with a fill or opacity change. Motion never carries a change alone: a colour,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">icon or label changes with it. </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`will-change`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> names only </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`transform`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`opacity`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> or </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`filter`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, and only</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">after a stutter you saw.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 38. Leave quieter than you arrive</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">The eye has moved on to what comes next; a big exit pulls it back.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Exits fade with a short fixed offset (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`y: -8`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> to </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`-12`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, blur 4px) in about 150ms, never a half-scale</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">shrink or a full-height flight. A drawer or sheet sliding back to its edge is the exception.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 39. Recolour icons with CSS, keep their stroke and grid</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">A thicker stroke, an off-grid size or a baked-in fill makes one glyph look like another set.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Icons paint with </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`currentColor`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; hover, selected and disabled are text colours and opacity. No</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`strokeWidth`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> on an instance: stroke comes in two tiers (rule 41). Sizes stay on the grid (12, 14, 16, 18,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">20, 24px). Arrows, chevrons, send and reply glyphs take </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`rtl:-scale-x-100`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; checkmarks, clocks and media</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">controls never flip.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 40. Name every icon-only button</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">A screen reader announces an unnamed icon button as "button" and nothing else.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`aria-label`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> on the button, with the same text as its tooltip. Icons beside a label stay hidden from</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">assistive tech (house icons already are).</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 41. Match the icon stroke to the text weight</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">A regular stroke beside semibold type looks faint, as if the icon were disabled.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Line icons have two stroke tiers: regular, and bold at 1.2x (rounded 1.75 → 2.1, outline 1.6 → 1.92).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Put </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`icon-bold`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> on semibold or bold text that holds icons (titles, Badge and section labels already</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">carry it), or </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`weight="bold"`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> on a lone icon; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`icon-regular`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> resets it inside. Medium text (buttons,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">menu rows) stays regular.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 42. Fill the active glyph where there's no row fill</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Colour alone is a weak signal for where you are, especially on a phone in sunlight.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Nav glyphs are outline at rest. In a tab bar, which has no row fill, the active item shows the filled</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">pair (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`HomeFilledIcon`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> for </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`HomeIcon`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) through </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`activeIcon`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">. Sidebars and rails mark the active row with</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">a fill, so their glyph stays outline.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">### 43. Align text to the reading direction</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`text-left`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> stays left in Arabic and Hebrew, where the text starts on the right.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`text-start`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> / </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`text-end`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, like </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`ps-*`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> / </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`pe-*`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> for padding. Physical alignment only where the content</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">itself is left to right (code gutters) or a stored setting is physical (an email block's alignment).</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">## References</span></span>
